    [System.Diagnostics.DebuggerDisplay("ReactiveUIBuilder")]
    public sealed class ReactiveUIBuilder : Splat.Builder.AppBuilder, ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIBuilder, ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIInstance
    {
        public ReactiveUIBuilder(Splat.IMutableDependencyResolver resolver, Splat.IReadonlyDependencyResolver? current) { }
        public ReactiveUI.ConverterService ConverterService { get; }
        public ReactiveUI.Primitives.Concurrency.ISequencer? MainThreadScheduler { get; }
        public ReactiveUI.Primitives.Concurrency.ISequencer? TaskpoolScheduler { get; }
        public ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIInstance Build() { }
        [System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImpl(System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
        public ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIInstance BuildApp() { }
        [System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImpl(System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
        public ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIBuilder ConfigureSuspensionDriver(System.Action<ReactiveUI.ISuspensionDriver> configure) { }
        [System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImpl(System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
        public ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIBuilder ConfigureViewLocator(System.Action<ReactiveUI.DefaultViewLocator> configure) { }
        [System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImpl(System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]
        public ReactiveUI.Builder.IReactiveUIBuilder ForCustomPlatform(ReactiveUI.Primitives.Concurrency.ISequencer mainThreadScheduler, System.Action<Splat.IMutableDependencyResolver> platformServices) { }

{
    public interface ISuspensionHost<TAppState> : ReactiveUI.ISuspensionHost
    {
        TAppState? AppStateValue { get; set; }

    }
